Driven by this limitation, they struggle to translate abstract numeric joint actions into coherent visual trajectories, and fail to predict dynamic visual changes from static initial observations. Consequently, successfully rendering an unseen embodiment zero-shot strictly requires heavily grounded cues, specifically pixel-space actions and explicit spatial-temporal alignment. Even when bypassing this zero-shot barrier via few-shot adaptation, the forced appearance recovery triggers catastrophic forgetting of seen embodiments. Together, these failures expose a critical inability to apply learned physical dynamics to novel visual appearances, highlighting that achieving true cross-embodiment generalization requires architectural innovations that decouple visual appearance from underlying physical dynamics.

</details>


### [269] [Coordinated Multi-Robot Disassembly for Makespan Optimization of Large-Scale Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05830)
*Niklas Hargus,Andreas Orthey,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoMuDi，一种面向多机器人拆卸任务的任务与运动规划方法。CoMuDi将机器人团队、物体装配和依赖图作为输入，通过创建复合任务（拾取、放置、退出运动）并传播时间约束来协调各机器人，确保其尽早开始和结束任务且避免碰撞。方法集成ST-RRT*路径规划器以最小化单任务到达时间并总体缩短完工时间。实验表明CoMuDi结合ST-RRT*相比RRT*具有更高成功率和更短完工时间，并在多达49个零件、9个机器人的六个装配场景中展现低空闲时间，证明其可可靠解决大规模装配拆卸问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人拆卸任务中，机器人需要在受限工作空间内协同运动，同时考虑与其他机器人的运动协调。现有方法难以高效处理此类任务与运动规划的组合问题，因此需要一种能够协调多个机器人在拆卸场景中协同工作的规划方法。

Method: 提出CoMuDi方法，输入为机器人团队、物体装配和依赖图。方法包括：(1) 基于依赖图创建拾取、放置和退出运动的复合任务；(2) 传播时间约束使每个机器人尽可能早地开始和结束任务；(3) 集成ST-RRT*规划器以确保单个任务优化到达时间，从而最小化整体makespan。

Result: 通过对比CoMuDi与ST-RRT*和RRT*在不同时间限制下的性能表现，证明CoMuDi与ST-RRT*的组合可获得更高成功率并最小化完工时间。在多达49个零件和9个机器人的六个装配场景中，CoMuDi返回的机器人路径展现出低空闲时间，可可靠解决大规模拆卸装配问题。

Conclusion: CoMuDi能够有效协调多机器人拆卸任务，通过时间约束传播与ST-RRT*集成实现低完工时间和高成功率，具备解决大规模装配场景的可靠性与扩展能力。

Abstract: Multi-robot task and motion planning for disassembly tasks requires robots to operate in confined workspaces while coordinating their motions with other robots. To tackle this problem, we propose a planning method called coordinated multi-robot disassembly (CoMuDi). CoMuDi coordinates a team of robots for disassembly tasks. The input is a team of robots, an assembly of objects, and a dependency graph. Based on this information, we create compound tasks for pick, place, and exit motions. By propagating temporal constraints, we ensure that each robot can start and end their tasks as early as possible while avoiding collisions with nearby robots. By integrating the space-time RRT* planner (ST-RRT*) into CoMuDi, we ensure that individual tasks minimize arrival time and thereby help us minimize overall makespan. We compare the performance of CoMuDi using both ST-RRT* and RRT* planners with varying time bounds, demonstrating that the combination of CoMuDi and ST-RRT* leads to a higher success rate while minimizing makespan. Finally, we evaluate CoMuDi on six assemblies with up to 49 pieces and up to 9 robots. In those scenarios, we show that CoMuDi returns robot paths that exhibit low idle times, thereby demonstrating that CoMuDi can reliably solve large-scale assemblies.

</details>


### [270] [SkillMemo: Expert-guided Skill Memory Framework for Compositional Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05970)
*Changyuan Wang,Chubin Zhang,Zhenyu Wu,Runhao Li,Angyuan Ma,Ke Chao,Yinan Liang,Xiuwei Xu,Ziwei Wang,Yansong Tang,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了SkillMemo框架，通过专家引导的轨迹分割和技能级情景记忆库，隐式地将长时序演示分解为潜在原子技能，并在推理时检索相关技能先验来增强DP和VLA模型的组合泛化能力，在模拟和真实世界任务中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身着形模型（如Diffusion Policy和VLA）在机器人操作基准上表现良好，但受限于大规模具身轨迹数据的稀缺，导致其在分布外场景中组合泛化能力不足，难以捕获可复用的技能结构。

Method: 1) 设计基于Mixture-of-Experts架构的专家引导轨迹分割模块，通过学习的门控系数隐式地将轨迹划分为不同的技能基元；2) 构建技能级情景记忆架构，以可检索的键值对形式存储紧凑的技能表示；3) 推理时从记忆库检索最相关的技能基元，与当前门控分布融合，提供上下文先验以细化动作预测。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实世界操作任务上的大量实验表明，SkillMemo一致性地提升了DP和VLA骨干模型的性能，达到SOTA水平，超越了π_{0.5}，并在未见过的任务配置中展现了强大的组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: SkillMemo通过隐式技能分解和动态情景记忆机制，有效缓解了具身数据稀缺问题，显著增强了针对组合任务的泛化能力，是提升现有具身着形模型性能的通用框架。

Abstract: Embodied visuomotor models, including Diffusion Policy (DP) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, have demonstrated promising performance on robotic manipulation benchmarks. However, their potential remains fundamentally constrained by the scarcity of large-scale embodied trajectory datasets, leading to insufficient compositional generalization in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios with limited capability to capture reusable skill structures. To address this limitation, we propose Skill-Based Memory (SkillMemo) framework that implicitly decomposes long-horizon demonstrations into latent atomic skills and integrates skill-level features into a dynamic episodic memory bank for solving compositional tasks. Specifically, we first introduce an expert-guided trajectory segmentation module built upon a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture, which implicitly partitions trajectories into distinct skill primitives represented by learned gating coefficients. We further design a skill-level episodic memory architecture that stores compact skill representations as retrievable key-value pairs. During inference, the memory bank retrieves the most relevant skill primitives which are subsequently fused with the model's current gating distribution, providing a robust contextual prior to refine action predictions. Extensive experiments on the simulation benchmark and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that SkillMemo consistently enhances both DP and VLA backbones, achieving state-of-the-art performance and outperforming $π_{0.5}$, while exhibiting strong compositional generalization to unseen task configurations.

</details>


### [271] [TRACE: Learned Proprioceptive Odometry for Legged Robots under Unreliable Contact Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05975)
*Taehyeon Kong,Woojin Kim,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一种端到端学习的足式机器人本体感觉里程计估计器，通过足部感知交叉注意力模块自适应加权IMU和腿部运动学token，在不可靠接触条件下实现稳健的相对位移、旋转和速度估计，并通过物理启发辅助损失、策略随机化及部分真实世界微调来提升sim-to-real迁移能力，在多种地形上相比基线方法显著降低位置漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人在不可靠接触条件下（如打滑、接触不稳固）的本体感觉里程计估计仍具挑战性。现有方法依赖人工定义的接触或滑移阈值，对复杂地形适应性差；同时纯学习方法存在策略过拟合和sim-to-real迁移困难的问题，导致实际部署中位置漂移显著。

Method: TRACE采用端到端架构，直接由惯导和关节测量历史预测相对位移、相对旋转和机体坐标系速度。核心创新为足部感知交叉注意力模块，以无阈值方式自适应加权IMU token和逐腿运动学token。训练包含直接监督与两个物理启发辅助损失（促进运动学一致性和腿部信息的可靠利用）。训练策略采用策略随机化提升泛化，随后对时间编码器和预测头进行部分真实世界微调。

Result: 在多种室内外地形实验中，TRACE相比经典的滤波方法、混合方法以及纯学习方法基线，一致性地降低了位置漂移。消融实验验证了所提训练目标、策略随机化和真实世界微调的有效性，尤其在不可靠接触和sim-to-real失配场景下贡献显著。

Conclusion: TRACE通过自适应足部感知注意力机制和针对性的训练策略，有效解决了不可靠接触条件下足式机器人里程计估计的鲁棒性问题，并缓解了sim-to-real迁移中的策略过拟合，为学习型本体感觉里程计提供了一种更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we present TRACE (Tokenized Robust Attention for Contact-Aware Estimation), an end-to-end learned proprioceptive odometry estimator for legged robots under unreliable contact conditions. The proposed estimator directly predicts relative displacement, relative rotation, and body-frame velocity from a recent history of onboard inertial and joint measurements. To improve robustness under unreliable contact conditions, we introduce a foot-aware cross-attention module that adaptively weights IMU and leg-wise kinematic tokens without relying on manually defined contact or slip thresholds. The estimator is trained with direct supervision and two physics-inspired auxiliary losses that promote kinematic consistency and reliable use of leg information. To reduce policy-specific overfitting and consequently improve sim-to-real transfer, simulation training incorporates policy randomization, followed by partial real-world fine-tuning of the temporal encoder and prediction head. Experiments across diverse indoor and outdoor terrains demonstrate consistent reductions in position drift compared with classical filtering-based, hybrid, and purely learning-based baselines. Ablation studies further validate the contributions of the proposed training objectives, policy randomization, and real-world fine-tuning, particularly under unreliable contacts and sim-to-real mismatch.

</details>


### [272] [Beyond Flat Policies: Hierarchical Post-Training for Embodied Agents in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05999)
*He Kong,Zengjue Chen,Qi Wang,Qianli Xing,Runliang Niu,Peidong Liu,Jiawei Li,Shiqi Wang,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiRoC提出一种分层后训练框架，将高层任务规划与低层动作执行解耦：规划器将复杂任务分解为可执行的子目标提供语义指导，执行器通过强化学习持续改进子目标条件下的动作生成，并通过子目标对齐缓解规划与执行之间的分布不匹配，在多个机器人操作基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的后训练方法主要将模型优化为扁平策略，难以显式建模任务进度并执行鲁棒的长程操作；而层次化方法虽引入任务分解，但依赖离线示范的监督学习，无法通过在线交互有效改进执行性能。

Method: 提出HiRoC层次化后训练框架，包含两个模块：高层规划器负责将复杂任务分解为可执行子目标，提供显式语义指导；低层执行器通过强化学习持续改进子目标条件下的动作生成。在强化学习之前，先对齐执行器与规划器生成的子目标，以缓解规划与执行之间的分布不匹配。

Result: 在多个机器人操作基准上的大量实验表明，HiRoC一致性地优于强基线方法。综合分析进一步验证了层次化后训练的有效性及各关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 层次化后训练框架通过解耦规划与执行，并借助强化学习进行在线优化，为VLA模型在复杂长程机器人操作任务中提供了更稳健、更可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in robotic manipulation by leveraging pretrained vision-language models. However, existing post-training methods predominantly optimize VLA models as flat policies, making it difficult to explicitly model task progression and perform robust long-horizon manipulation. Although hierarchical approaches introduce task decomposition, they mainly rely on supervised learning from offline demonstrations and cannot effectively improve execution through online interaction. To address this limitation, we propose Hierarchical Robotic Control (HiRoC), a hierarchical post-training framework that decouples high-level task planning from low-level action execution. The planner decomposes complex tasks into executable subgoals to provide explicit semantic guidance, while the executor continuously improves subgoal-conditioned action generation through reinforcement learning. To enable effective collaboration between the two modules, we further align the executor with planner-generated subgoals before reinforcement learning, mitigating the distribution misalignment between planning and execution. Extensive experiments across diverse robotic manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that HiRoC consistently outperforms strong baselines. Comprehensive analyses further validate the effectiveness of hierarchical post-training and the contribution of each key component.

</details>


### [273] [Adaptive-WAM: Quality-Guided Early-Exit Planning from Intermediate Video-Diffusion Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06008)
*Sining Ang,Yuguang Yang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Adaptive-WAM，一种基于视频扩散模型的多出口轨迹规划器。核心发现是：规划性能对视频去噪步数和DiT深度不敏感，强轨迹可从中间层直接解码。据此，Adaptive-WAM在DiT块上附加轨迹扩散头，通过轻量级质量评分器自适应决定推理时机，避免迭代去噪和VAE解码，在NAVSIM上达90.8 PDMS，推理延迟较固定全深度规划器降低47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视频扩散模型为自动驾驶提供了丰富的时空先验，但现有world-action模型即使部署时仅需自车轨迹，仍继承了迭代未来视频生成的昂贵计算成本。作者提出更基本的问题：执行一个视频扩散模型的多少部分才能做出可靠的驾驶决策？旨在消除不必要的视频合成计算，实现高效规划。

Method: 首先进行受控研究，系统地考察视频去噪时间步和DiT深度对规划性能的影响，发现性能对噪声水平不敏感、中间层即可解码强轨迹。基于此观察，构建Adaptive-WAM：基于Wan2.2-5B骨干网络，在选定的DiT块上附加轨迹扩散头，并部署轻量级轨迹质量评分器——一旦当前解码的最佳轨迹满足质量阈值即终止推理，否则从缓存的隐藏状态继续到更深出口。该设计绕过了迭代无分类器去噪循环和VAE解码。

Result: 在NAVSIM上自适应单轨迹规划器达到90.8 PDMS；固定出口变体以64个候选轨迹达92.6 PDMS；在NAVSIM v2上获89.9 EPDMS，为所比较的前视视频世界模型规划器中最佳。无需目标域微调即可迁移至nuScenes（平均L2误差0.88m，碰撞率0.08%）。在A100上，自适应路由将PDMS从90.62提升至90.79，端到端规划延迟平均170ms，比固定块15规划器（190ms）低约10%，比固定全深度规划器（320ms）低47%。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了视频扩散模型在规划任务中的计算冗余性，证明通过质量感知的自适应多出口机制可以大幅削减推理开销，同时保持甚至提升规划性能。该工作为高效world-action模型提供了新范式，即用更少的生成计算达成可靠的驾驶决策。

Abstract: Large video diffusion models provide rich spatiotemporal priors for autonomous driving, but existing world-action models often inherit the cost of iterative future-video generation even though deployment only requires an ego trajectory. We ask a more basic question: how much of a video diffusion model must be executed to make a reliable driving decision? Through a controlled study of video denoising timesteps and Diffusion Transformer (DiT) depth, we find that planning performance is largely insensitive to the tested video-noise levels, whereas strong trajectories can already be decoded from intermediate layers. Based on this observation, we introduce Adaptive-WAM, a quality-aware multi-exit planner built on a Wan2.2-5B backbone. Trajectory diffusion heads are attached to selected DiT blocks, and a lightweight trajectory-quality scorer terminates inference once the best trajectory decoded so far satisfies a quality threshold; otherwise, computation continues from the cached hidden state to a deeper exit. The deployed planner therefore avoids the iterative classifier-free denoising loop and VAE decoding required for future-video synthesis, while dynamically allocating backbone depth according to trajectory quality. On NAVSIM, the adaptive single-trajectory planner achieves 90.8 PDMS; a separate fixed-exit variant reaches 92.6 PDMS with 64 proposals. It further obtains 89.9 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, yielding the best reported results among the compared front-view video world-model planners. Without target-domain fine-tuning, Adaptive-WAM transfers to nuScenes with 0.88 m average L2 error and a 0.08\% collision rate. On an A100, adaptive routing improves PDMS from 90.62 to 90.79 while averaging 170 ms end-to-end planning latency, approximately 10\% below the 190 ms fixed block-15 planner and 47\% below the 320 ms fixed full-depth planner. Code will be released.

</details>


### [274] [Topometric Autonomous Vehicle Localization by Combining Visual Embeddings and Feed-Forward 3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06021)
*Eulogio Quemada-Torres,Alberto Jaenal,Francisco-Angel Moreno,Javier Gonzalez-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种顶度量（topometric）框架，通过结合概率视觉位置识别（VPR）与前馈神经3D几何（FF3D）模型的度量姿态估计，实现顺序外观定位。该框架包含离线自动建图工具和在线粒子滤波器，在三个基准上显著优于现有外观方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉定位需要兼顾紧凑性、对外观变化的鲁棒性和度量精度。VPR通过低维嵌入满足前两点但度量精度低；传统基于局部特征或神经表示的VL方法精度高但扩展性差。作者旨在融合两者的优势，通过集成VPR与FF3D度量估计来克服各自局限。

Method: 提出一个顶度量框架：(1) 离线阶段，通过自动建图工具建模场景不同区域的姿态-外观交互关系；(2) 在线阶段，使用粒子滤波器融合里程计和地点置信度进行姿态估计，并利用地点置信度触发FF3D推理，将神经度量估计纳入概率外观定位框架。描述符提取器和FF3D模型均可互换。

Result: 在三个已知基准上进行广泛评估，结果显著优于现有外观定位方法。进一步的分析表明，顺序置信度（sequential belief）能在感知混淆（perceptual aliasing）下有效缓解严重失败。

Conclusion: 通过将概率VPR与FF3D度量姿态估计有机融合于顶度量框架中，实现了兼具可扩展性、外观鲁棒性和度量精度的视觉定位，且系统模块化设计保证了组件的可替换性。

Abstract: Effective Visual Localization (VL) requires a map of the environment that combines compactness for efficient scalability with robustness against visual appearance changes and metric precision. Through low-dimensional image embeddings, Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is able to successfully meet the first two requirements, but its low metric accuracy makes it less suitable than standard VL approaches based on local features or neural representations. This limitation can be overcome by integrating VPR with the accurate local trajectory estimates produced by feed-forward neural 3D geometry (FF3D) models. In this paper, we address sequential appearance-based localization through a topometric framework that iteratively combines probabilistic VPR with FF3D metric pose estimation in controlled image sets. Our approach proposes an automatic offline mapping tool that models the topometric pose-appearance interaction in the different parts of the scene. This map is later employed by an online particle filter that estimates the pose from odometry and belief over places for FF3D inference, successfully incorporating neural metric estimation into probabilistic appearance-based localization. We extensively evaluate the framework on three known benchmarks, demonstrating substantial improvements over existing appearance-based methods. The modularity of our approach allows the descriptor extractor and FF3D model to remain interchangeable, and a focused analysis further shows that sequential belief can mitigate severe failures under perceptual aliasing.

</details>


### [275] [IcFuzz: Fuzzing Isaac Sim with Semantic Stage Guidance and Multi-level Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06088)
*Zhixiang Chen,Zhuangbin Chen,Ruoxi Jia,Zeqin Liao,Wei Li,Jinyang Liu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出IcFuzz，首个针对NVIDIA Isaac Sim仿真器的模糊测试方法。通过基于LLM的语义阶段分割、多级别变异算子设计以及多臂老虎机算法自适应调度，IcFuzz显著提升了代码覆盖率和缺陷检测能力，共发现11个bug，其中9个已被开发者确认或修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVIDIA Isaac Sim作为机器人仿真基础设施，其复杂性不可避免引入软件缺陷。现有模糊测试方法因难以处理上下文相关的对象语义、层次化的仿真控制以及庞大的仿真状态空间，无法有效测试Isaac Sim，亟需新的测试方法。

Method: IcFuzz包含三个核心组件：1) 基于LLM的语义阶段分割，将仿真程序分解为结构化阶段以捕获上下文感知的对象语义；2) 多级别变异算子设计，从层次化粒度系统性地测试仿真器；3) 多臂老虎机算法，用于自适应调度变异算子以高效遍历庞大的仿真状态空间。

Result: 实验结果表明，IcFuzz在代码覆盖率上达到基线方法的190%-205%；在三轮12小时测试中平均检测到3.7个独特崩溃，而基线方法未检测到任何崩溃。此外，约四个月内共发现11个bug，其中9个已被开发者确认或修复。

Conclusion: IcFuzz通过结合LLM语义分析和自适应调度策略，有效解决了Isaac Sim仿真器模糊测试中的关键挑战，验证了该方法在提高仿真器可靠性和缺陷检测效率方面的显著优势。

Abstract: Robotics simulators serve as a foundational infrastructure for embodied AI, facilitating safe and scalable robotic system development. NVIDIA Isaac Sim has emerged as one of the most popular simulators, distinguished by its GPU-accelerated physics engine and photorealistic rendering, which enable high-fidelity modeling of complex environments. However, its inherent complexity inevitably introduces software bugs that can compromise simulation reliability. Existing fuzzing approaches struggle to test Isaac Sim effectively due to challenges of context-aware object semantics, hierarchical simulation control, and a vast simulation state space.
  In this paper, we propose IcFuzz, the first fuzzing approach for Isaac Sim. IcFuzz first performs an LLM-based semantic stage segmentation, decomposing simulation programs into structured stages that capture context-aware object semantics. Guided by this information, IcFuzz designs multi-level mutation operators to systematically exercise the simulator across hierarchical granularities. To efficiently navigate the vast simulation state space, IcFuzz employs a multi-armed bandit algorithm to adaptively schedule mutation operators. Experimental results show that IcFuzz outperforms the baselines in terms of both code coverage and bug detection. Specifically, IcFuzz achieves approximately 190\%--205\% of the code coverage of the baselines and detects an average of 3.7 unique crashes over three rounds of 12-hour tests, while no crashes are detected by the baselines. Moreover, IcFuzz has uncovered 11 bugs over approximately four months, 9 of which have been confirmed or fixed by the developers.

</details>


### [276] [Visual Grounding in Zero-Shot Vision-Language Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06154)
*J. de Curtò,Dayani Plasencia,Diego Sánchez,I. de Zarzà*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过输入消融测试（盲图、重复输入、镜像反射等）系统评估了VLM作为零样本控制器的视觉基础性。结果显示直接控制性能普遍不佳，但确定性视觉正控表明刺激携带足够信息；通过泄漏控制的对称一致性监护人机制，在272帧测试上达到0.954平衡准确率，支持VLM作为有界、选择性的危险辅助工具，而非一体化零样本控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs被用作零样本控制器时，成功轨迹未必证明决策基于视觉输入——模拟器动力学和保守动作先验可能在没有有意义感知的情况下产生良好分数。作者希望严谨区分"看起来有效"和"真正视觉落地"的控制器行为。

Method: 设计了输入消融测试组（盲图控制、重复相同输入、车道轴反射、非视觉基线和流程完整性检查），覆盖9个直接动作模型、6个结构化局部VLM和VLM-MPC层级。构建泄漏控制的对称一致性监护人机制，在16个校准帧上选择模型并固定2-of-4风险投票规则，在272帧上进行评估；同时使用嵌套留一情节交叉验证。

Result: 直接控制结果总体消极：恒定SLOW策略超过脚本化几何控制器；多模型对图像不变或近恒定；能识别纵向危险的模型在反射下无法转换LEFT/RIGHT。影像仅确定性正控以0.090m MAE估计前车间距且精确镜像等变。监护人机制在272帧上达0.954平衡准确率（95%CI [0.895,0.990]），留一验证12折均恢复相同模型对和阈值；弃权策略将承诺平衡准确率提升至0.973（覆盖率0.824）；离线模块化重放达成0.934动作一致性和精确镜像等变。

Conclusion: 当前VLM的视觉接地是有界且选择性的，适合作为危险辅助工具使用，而非独立的端到端零样本控制器。失败是模块化的而非普遍的，通过合适的模块化结构和防护机制（如同对称一致性验证）可以弥补单独VLM的不足。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as zero-shot controllers, but successful trajectories do not necessarily show that decisions are grounded in visual input: simulator dynamics and conservative action priors can produce favourable scores without meaningful perception. We investigate this with an input-ablation battery: blind-image controls, repeated identical inputs, lane-axis reflection, non-visual baselines, and pipeline-integrity checks. Across nine direct-action models, six structured local VLMs, and an exploratory VLM-MPC hierarchy, we analyse 32,874 scored calls over two embodiments and three simulators. The direct-control results are largely negative: a constant-SLOW policy outperforms a scripted geometric controller, several models are image-invariant or nearly constant, and models that recognize longitudinal hazards still fail to transform LEFT and RIGHT under reflection. No local VLM meets the joint longitudinal and lateral grounding criteria. However, an image-only deterministic positive control estimates the lead gap with 0.090 m MAE and exact mirror equivariance, confirming the stimuli carry sufficient visual information; the failures are modular, not universal. A post-hoc, leakage-controlled symmetry-consensus guardian selects two models from 16 calibration frames and freezes a 2-of-4 hazard vote across original and reflected views. On 272 held-out frames it reaches 0.954 balanced accuracy (episode-cluster bootstrap 95% CI [0.895,0.990]); nested leave-one-episode-out recovers the same pair and threshold in all 12 folds. Abstaining on ties raises committed balanced accuracy to 0.973 at 0.824 coverage. With deterministic perception retaining lateral authority, offline modular replay achieves 0.934 action agreement and exact mirror equivariance. These results support current VLMs as bounded, selective hazard assistants, not monolithic zero-shot controllers.

</details>


### [277] [Prior-SG: Task and Prior Driven Region Segmentation for Scene Graphs in Arbitrarily-Structured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06170)
*Giorgio Tonetti,Laurent Kneip,Abel Gawel,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Prior-SG是一个任务与先验驱动的层次化3D场景图生成框架，将场景图生成建模为概率对齐问题，通过MAP估计和MRF优化融合视觉、几何、物体等多源专家信息，并利用LLM动态合成先验图来指导高层功能语义推断，在开放平面环境中取得SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图提取框架依赖纯局部视觉聚类或严格几何启发式（如墙隔房间），在开放式平面或任意结构环境中失效，难以支持高层空间推理，缺乏任务驱动的语义灵活性。

Method: 首先将场景图生成建模为概率对齐问题；机器人探索时通过多尺度开放词汇特征融合策略将RGB-D传感器流聚合成物理实例图；然后利用LLM动态合成先验图（逻辑环境结构期望和任务相关词汇），通过MAP估计推断高层功能语义；最后通过优化MRF融合视觉、几何、离散物体等异构专家信息与拓扑先验，解决局部感知歧义。

Result: 在多种模拟住宅数据集和大规模开放平面真实环境中验证，Prior-SG在语义区域分割精度上达到SOTA，能在无物理墙的情况下稳健划分远处功能边界，并实现零样本本体灵活性，可根据高层任务完全重构空间分区。

Conclusion: 先验与任务驱动的概率对齐框架比纯几何启发式方法更适合开放平面及任意结构环境下的层次化3D场景图生成，能有效结合LLM先验与多源感知信息实现鲁棒且灵活的功能语义推断。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D scene graphs are a promising representation for high-level spatial reasoning in autonomous mobile platforms. However, existing extraction frameworks typically rely on purely local visual clustering or strict geometric heuristics, such as wall-separated rooms, which fail in open-plan or arbitrarily-structured environments. We propose Prior-SG, a task- and prior-driven framework that casts scene graph generation fundamentally as a probabilistic alignment problem. As the robot explores, it continuously aggregates an incoming RGB-D sensor stream into a physically grounded Instance Graph utilizing a multi-scale, open-vocabulary feature fusion strategy. The system then infers the high-level functional semantics of this map through a Maximum A Posteriori (MAP) estimate, guided by a Prior Graph-a logical expectation of the environment's structure and task-relevant vocabulary synthesized dynamically by a Large Language Model. By optimizing a Markov Random Field that fuses heterogeneous experts (visual, geometric, and discrete objects) with these topological priors, the system resolves local perceptual ambiguities. We validate this approach across diverse simulated residential datasets and large, open-plan real-world environments. Prior-SG achieves state-of-the-art semantic region segmentation accuracy compared to recent baselines, robustly delineates distant functional boundaries in the absence of physical walls, and uniquely provides zero-shot ontological flexibility, enabling the robot to entirely restructure its spatial partitioning based on a given high-level task.

</details>


### [278] [Robot Learning from Human Demonstrations: Handwritten Alphabet Trajectories and Human-Likeness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06221)
*Alperen Kenan,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于示教学习（LfD）的框架，用于生成类人机器人运动，包括数据采集、概率轨迹学习与感知评估。通过触摸屏遥操作采集了22名参与者的3,142条手写演示数据（覆盖52个拉丁字母大小写组合），在GMM/GMR方法基础上引入力与归一化时间维度，并适配非连续多段轨迹。用户研究（21人）显示生成轨迹的类人评分达71.50（SD=22.56），表明大多数轨迹被感知为更类人，且参与者对类人机器人行为持积极态度。数据集已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人示教学习通过观察和模仿人类动力学来发展运动技能，减少显式编程依赖。类人运动被视为建立信任和自然协作的关键因素。现有基于GMM/GMR的LfD方法在处理包含力和时序信息的多段非连续轨迹方面存在不足，且缺乏系统的用户感知评估与可复现基准。

Method: 1) 数据采集：通过触摸屏遥操作界面收集22名参与者的3,142条手写演示（52种字母大小写组合），记录平面位置、接触力和时序。2) 轨迹学习：扩展GMM/GMR方法，引入力与归一化时间维度，并处理非连续多段轨迹以实现跨演示的泛化。3) 感知评估：21名参与者的用户研究，采用0-100连续量表（50为中性中点）评估轨迹的类人程度。

Result: 生成轨迹整体类人评分为71.50（SD=22.56），多数轨迹被认为更类人。参与者指出几何定位和轨迹序列是最具影响力的感知因素，并对类人机器人行为持积极态度。数据集已开源发布。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了在LfD中融入力与时间维度、处理多段轨迹后能够生成被用户感知为更具类人特征的运动，且用户对类人机器人行为持积极态度。开源数据集为类人机器人运动方法的开发与评估提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) provides a developmental framework through which robots can develop motor skills by observing and imitating human dynamics, reducing reliance on explicit programming to teach a skill to a robot. The resulting human-like robot motion is recognised as a key factor in building trust and enabling natural collaboration in human-robot interaction. This paper presents a framework for learning human-like robot motion from demonstration, including data collection, probabilistic trajectory learning, and perceptual user evaluation. A dataset of 3,142 handwriting demonstrations was collected from 22 participants across all 52 Latin alphabet character-case combinations via a touchscreen teleoperation interface, capturing planar position, contact force, and timing. Building on the widely used Gaussian Mixture Model and Gaussian Mixture Regression approach for learning from demonstration, the framework is extended in this work by incorporating force and normalised time dimensions to enable richer representation of human dynamics, and adapting it to handle non-continuous, multi-segment trajectories, enabling generalisation across demonstrations. A user study with 21 participants evaluated the perceived human-likeness of the generated trajectories using a continuous scale anchored between robotic and human-like motion, normalised to 0-100 where 50 represents the neutral midpoint. The generated trajectories achieved an overall human-likeness score of 71.50 (SD=22.56), indicating that the majority of trajectories were perceived as more human-like. Participants identified geometric positioning and trajectory sequence as the most influential perceptual factors, and reported positive attitudes toward human-like robot behaviour. The datasets are released as open-source, providing a reproducible benchmark for developing and evaluating human-like robot motion methods.

</details>


### [279] [GeniWorld: A Generalizable Interactive World Model for Robotic Manipulation via Visual Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06332)
*Chenghao Gu,Hanyang Yu,Jingbo Zhang,Haitao Lin,Wenyao Zhang,Jinghe Wang,Hanglei Jin,Shuzhao Xie,Jingyan Jiang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeniWorld是一个基于预训练视频生成模型的交互式世界模型，通过URDF渲染将数值动作转化为视觉动作表征，解耦本体运动学与环境动力学，实现跨未见场景的零样本泛化，并可作为策略评估器与数据生成器提升下游策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在复杂未见环境中的鲁棒性有限，且多样化真实环境的规模化学习与评估成本高昂。现存的基于动作条件的世界模型在动作可控性和分布外场景泛化上表现不佳。

Method: 1) 基于预训练视频生成模型构建世界模型；2) 利用URDF渲染将数值动作转化为视觉动作表征，实现空间锚定的动作控制；3) 显式解耦本体运动学与环境动力学，缓解场景过拟合；4) 构建自回归视频预测模型并与高频机器人运动控制集成，实现闭环控制。

Result: 在仅使用有限固定场景数据训练的情况下，模型在领域内性能优于基线，并对高度随机的未见环境实现鲁棒零样本泛化。此外，GeniWorld可作为可扩展策略评估器在环境扰动下保持可靠，并能在有限真实演示下生成多样化操作轨迹以提升下游策略性能。

Conclusion: GeniWorld通过解耦本体与环境的建模策略和基于URDF的视觉动作表征，有效解决了世界模型在动作可控性和OOD泛化上的瓶颈，为通用机器人策略评估与数据扩充提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies exhibit strong capabilities, but their robustness in complex and unseen environments remains limited. Scaling robot learning and evaluation in diverse real-world environments remains costly and challenging. Action-conditioned world models offer a promising alternative, but they often suffer from limited action controllability and poor generalization to out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To this end, we present GeniWorld, an interactive world model for robots that generalizes robustly across unseen scenarios. Building on pretrained video generative models, we use URDF-based rendering to transform numerical actions into visual action representations, enabling spatially grounded action control. By explicitly decoupling embodiment kinematics from environmental dynamics, our model mitigates scene overfitting and facilitates modeling of robot-environment interactions. To achieve closed-loop control, we construct an autoregressive video prediction model integrated with high-frequency robot kinematic control, enabling interaction with both robot policies and human teleoperators. In our experiments, even when trained solely on limited fixed-scene data, our model achieves superior in-domain performance and robust zero-shot generalization to highly randomized, unseen environments. For downstream applications, GeniWorld serves as a scalable policy evaluator that remains reliable under environmental perturbations. Furthermore, even with limited real-world demonstrations, GeniWorld generates diverse manipulation trajectories within the world model, improving downstream policy performance and robustness in complex environments.

</details>


### [280] [A Master-Salve Robot Manipulator for Needle-Based Teleoperation in MRI Chamber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06354)
*Omar Curiel,Jing-Yuan Huang,Po-Chih Chen,Ji Ma,Qing Dai,Wenqi Zhou,David Lu,Holden H. Wu,Tsu-Chin Tsao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种MRI安全的主从式机器人操控器，用于MRI腔内的腹部介入手术，通过流体传动实现2+1自由度运动传递，支持手动、数字、混合和协作四种控制模式，并在初步猪体内实验中验证了床边手动控制模式的功能可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI引导下的腹部介入手术需要MR安全的机器人操控系统，而现有研究中的分离轴或模式切换式混合人机控制架构存在控制模式局限，无法充分发挥多模态控制能力。

Method: 采用主从式双操控器设计：人工操作的2+1自由度主操控器通过流体传动将运动和力传递给2+1自由度从操控器；高输入阻抗、低泄漏的弹性体流体执行器负责远端角度控制，低摩擦石墨活塞气缸负责针插入轴的远程驱动（具备亚牛顿力透明度和亚毫米级运动传递）；数字主控制器提供超越传统架构的多模态控制能力，支持手动、数字、混合和协作四种控制模式。

Result: 初步MRI扫描仪结果证明了操控器在床边手动控制模式下对活体猪实验的功能可行性；协作任务（包括辅助组织穿透、故障驱动虚拟夹具和反馈控制的运动补偿）已在论文中验证。

Conclusion: 该MR安全主从式操控器实现了手动、数字、混合及协作四种控制模式，通过流体传动设计兼顾了MR兼容性与多模态控制能力，并在活体实验中初步验证了其临床可用性。

Abstract: We present a MR safe, master-slave robot manipulator for abdominal interventions in the MRI chamber. A human operated 2+1-DoF master controller manipulator transmits motion and force to a 2+1-DoF slave manipulator via fluid transmission. Jointly, a digital master controller provides multimodal control capability beyond common split axis or mode switchable hybrid human-digital controller configurations found in previous studies. High input impedance, low-leakage, elastomeric fluid actuators are delegated to remote angulation control. Low-friction graphite piston cylinders are delegated to needle insertion axis remote actuation given the sub-newton force transparency and sub-millimeter motion transmission over bedside fluid piping lengths. The device enables real-time MRI guided interventions allowing manual, digital, hybrid, and collaborative control modes. Collaborative tasks such as assisted tissue penetration, fault-driven virtual fixture, and motion compensation through feedback control are presented in this paper. Preliminary MR scanner results demonstrate manipulator functional viability for an in-vivo pig experiment in bedside, manual control mode configuration.

</details>


### [281] [DyPES-VLA: Learning Shared Dynamics Priors and Embodiment-Specific Control for Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06374)
*Junfeng Li,Junjie He,Zhide Zhong,Yangyang Zheng,Pingyue Sheng,Jiayu Dong,Ruixin Li,Haodong Yan,Jiaguan Zhu,Tianran Zhang,Runze Yu,Wen Chen,Liuqing Yang,Yuxiang Gao,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DyPES-VLA，一种跨本体（cross-embodiment）的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型。它通过在未来预测目标上训练VLM来学习共享的动力学先验，并使用具身专属的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）动作头直接在各自原生动作空间中生成可执行控制，避免了繁琐的人工动作对齐预处理。在LIBERO、RoboCasa-GR1和RoboTwin 2.0上均取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨本体VLA模型存在两个主要局限：(1) 未能充分利用不同视觉与交互数据中共享的动力学先验，限制了跨本体的迁移能力；(2) 需要大量人工预处理将各本体的动作转化为统一格式，成本高昂且不灵活。

Method: DyPES-VLA包含两个核心组件：(1) 共享动力学先验学习：通过在跨本体数据上使用未来预测目标训练VLM，使共享的query表示能够捕获物体运动、接触以及交互引起的场景变化；(2) 具身专属MoE动作头：由共享的注意力层负责捕获常见的时间动作结构，而具身专属的前馈专家负责解析不同本体的运动学约束和控制语义，从而直接在各本体的原生动作空间中输出可执行控制，无需人工预对齐。

Result: 作为通用策略，DyPES-VLA在仿真和真实世界评估中均取得最先进性能：LIBERO上达到98.0%成功率，RoboCasa-GR1上达到59.25%，RoboTwin 2.0上达到89.02%。

Conclusion: DyPES-VLA通过解耦"共享动力学先验"与"具身专属控制"，有效解决了异构机器人本体的通用策略训练难题，验证了该方法在跨本体迁移和动作生成方面的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a powerful paradigm for robot manipulation, but training a single generalist policy for heterogeneous robot embodiments remains an open problem. Existing methods have two main limitations. First, they underuse dynamics priors shared across diverse visual and interaction data, limiting cross-embodiment transfer. Second, they require extensive manual preprocessing to convert embodiment-specific actions into a common format. To overcome these limitations, we propose DyPES-VLA, a cross-embodiment VLA that learns shared Dynamics Priors and Embodiment-Specific control. First, we learn shared dynamics priors by training the vision-language model (VLM) with a future-prediction objective on cross-embodiment data, driving the shared query representation to capture object motion, contact, and interaction-induced scene changes. Second, an embodiment-specific Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) action head translates these shared dynamics priors into executable controls directly in each embodiment's native action space, without manually pre-aligning heterogeneous actions into a common format. This head shares attention layers to capture common temporal action structures, while its embodiment-specific feed-forward experts resolve the unique kinematic constraints and control semantics of distinct embodiments. As a generalist policy, our \ourmethod achieves state-of-the-art performance across simulation and real-world evaluations, reaching 98.0% success on LIBERO, 59.25% on RoboCasa-GR1, and 89.02% on RoboTwin~2.0.

</details>


### [282] [$ω$-0: A Latent Predictive World Action Model for Concurrent Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06375)
*Zhe Li,Zhenzhe Zhang,Yangyang Wei,Wenjie Zhang,Xichen Yuan,Peiyuan Zhi,Gen Li,Xinying Guo,Fengjie Gao,Jianfei Yang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ω-0，一个用于真实世界人形机器人并发移动-操作（loco-manipulation）任务的潜在预测全身世界动作模型。它以语言指令、视觉观测和本体感知状态为输入，直接预测控制器兼容的全身动作潜在表示，通过轻量级潜在视觉预测目标耦合扩散式全身动作生成。在11个家务任务上的真实实验表明，单一ω-0模型能产生平滑的"边移动边操作"行为，并持续优于各类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人家务任务往往需要同时进行移动与操作（loco-manipulation），要求机器人协调运动、调整姿态、保持平衡并操作物体。然而现有的人形策略通常将移动和操作分解为独立模块，而近期基于世界动作模型的方案要么以手臂为中心、要么以视频为中心，难以支持真正的全身并发协调行为。

Method: ω-0是一个潜在预测的全身世界动作模型。其核心方法包括：1) 直接预测控制器兼容的全身动作潜在表示，而非重构未来视频；2) 学习紧凑的未来观测嵌入作为轻量级预测目标，将潜在视觉前瞻与基于扩散模型的全身动作生成耦合；3) 支持第一人称RGB、第三人称RGB和第三人称深度多种观测输入；4) 利用基于控制器的仿真回放，将人类/公共视觉-运动先验扎根为机器人可执行的动作潜在表示；5) 收集了ω-HOME数据集，包含40+小时的多视图观测、全身SMPL运动、机器人状态和动作潜在表示。

Result: 在11个真实世界家务任务上的实验表明，单一ω-0模型能够产生平滑的移动-操作并发行为，并一致性地优于代表性的模仿学习、视觉语言动作模型（VLA）、人形机器人和世界动作模型（WAM）基线。

Conclusion: ω-0证明了在真实世界中通过潜在预测世界动作模型实现人形机器人全身并发移动-操作是可行且有效的，为后续人形机器人全身协同控制提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Humanoid household tasks often require concurrent loco-manipulation, where the robot must move, adjust posture, maintain balance, and manipulate objects as a single coordinated behavior. Yet existing humanoid policies typically decompose locomotion and manipulation, while recent world-action models remain either arm-centric or video-centered. We present $ω$-0, a latent predictive whole-body world-action model for real-world humanoid concurrent loco-manipulation. Given a language instruction, current visual observation, and robot proprioceptive state, $ω$-0 directly predicts controller-compatible whole-body action latents for real-robot execution. Rather than reconstructing future videos, $ω$-0 learns compact future observation embeddings as a lightweight predictive objective, coupling latent visual foresight with diffusion-based whole-body action generation. The model supports egocentric RGB, exocentric RGB, and exocentric depth inputs, and leverages controller-based simulation replay to ground human/public visual-motion priors into robot-executable action latents. We further collect $ω$-HOME, a 40+ hour real-world household humanoid dataset with synchronized multi-view observations, whole-body SMPL motions, robot states, and action latents. Real-world experiments on 11 household tasks demonstrate that a single $ω$-0 model can produce smooth manipulate-while-moving behaviors and consistently outperform representative imitation learning, VLA, humanoid, and WAM baselines.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [283] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MS-MLB 是一个基于 GSE17048 全血 RNA 表达数据的多发性硬化症（MS）机器学习分类基准，通过共享的防泄漏评估流水线评估多种算法，梯度提升在保留测试集上取得最佳 MS Research Score（93.83，AUC-ROC 0.989）。该基准仅供研究比较，未经临床验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发性硬化症的诊断依赖临床评估、影像和实验室检查，血液 RNA 表达数据可能包含疾病相关的免疫信号，但缺少一个可复现、防数据泄漏的开源基准来评估基于全血 RNA 的 MS 机器学习分类模型。

Method: 使用公开的 GSE17048 队列，构造 MS 与健康对照的二分类任务；在统一的防泄漏流水线下评估多种机器学习算法，包括嵌套交叉验证、未触碰的保留测试集、bootstrap 置信区间、ROC/PR 分析、校准度量，并引入探索性的 MS Research Score，同时提供外部模型提交路径。

Result: 梯度提升算法在保留测试集上排名第一，MS Research Score 为 93.83，AUC-ROC 为 0.989，灵敏度 0.950，特异度 0.778，F1 分数 0.927，Brier 分数 0.050。

Conclusion: MS-MLB 是首个针对 GSE17048 全血 RNA 数据、聚焦 MS 与健康对照分类并内置文档化外部模型提交路径的开源基准；其分数仅用于研究比较，未经临床验证，不能替代临床诊断。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [284] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFTER框架，通过挖掘冻结预测器的残差来发现可解释的纠错特征，用轻量级事后修正器提升预测性能，无需微调骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测器常常以结构化、可重复的方式失败，通过微调修复代价高昂。与以往建模数据生成过程的自动特征工程不同，本文转向建模"模型失败过程"，即在冻结模型的残差中发现可解释特征来驱动轻量级事后修正，从而避免昂贵的微调成本。

Method: CRAFTER保持骨干网络冻结，通过两个互补的生成器挖掘残差特征：(1) 基于原始输入通道的组合搜索；(2) 大语言模型（LLM）提出命名特征组合、二元标志和简短可执行代码。所有候选特征（无论来源）都经过一个基于验证集的统一门控来接受或拒绝，最终由验证集选定的修正器应用接受的特征或保持预测不变。这种与来源无关的流水线还允许先前特征工程系统在相同条件下评估，从而将预测改进归因于特征来源本身。

Result: 在六个公开数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上，CRAFTER在每个特征预算下都超越了所有专用特征工程系统，其改进约为仅用修正器时的两倍，并将最弱骨干网络的错误率降低高达27%。这些增益在不同LLM后端下均保持稳健，并且在微调后的骨干网络上仍然有效。

Conclusion: CRAFTER成功地将特征工程从"数据生成过程"范式转向"模型失败过程"范式，通过来源无关的流水线实现了对冻结预测器残差的可解释纠错特征发现，为低成本修复预训练模型提供了新思路，同时为特征工程系统的公平比较提供了统一平台。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [285] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于编程LLM流程的概率性语言，使开发者能够量化和传播整个应用流程中的不确定性，并在不额外编写代码的情况下试验不同的推理缩放技术。通过实验研究和Rocq定理证明器的案例研究验证了该能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可靠的大语言模型（LLM）应用仍面临重大挑战：LLM输出缺乏准确性和置信度度量，且这种不确定性在多次LLM调用和其他工具组成的流程中会叠加放大，使开发者和最终用户难以信任结果。

Method: 论文引入了一种概率语言用于编程基于LLM的流程，使开发者能够在整个应用流程中量化和传播不确定性，并能试验不同的推理缩放技术，而无需在流程逻辑之外增加任何代码。

Result: 论文进行了实验研究以证明该能力，并构建了一个针对Rocq定理证明器的定理证明智能体案例研究。

Conclusion: 该概率语言提供了一种有效的方案来解决LLM应用中的不确定性问题，使开发者能够更可靠地构建和调试基于LLM的复杂流程。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [286] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGTime，一个4B参数的计算接地时间序列-语言模型，通过将感知（确定性计算统计量）与描述（LLM言语化预计算结果）解耦，解决了多模态时间序列-语言对齐中自监督陷阱导致的"可靠、现实、可扩展"三难问题，在多元时间序列理解任务上超越了更大的通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练多模态模型对齐时间序列与语言面临两个问题：(1) 自监督陷阱——让LLM读取序列并写描述，标签质量受限于模型自身的感知能力，数据永远无法教会超过标注者已知的内容；(2) 大多数数据集仅使用单变量，但关键的跨通道相关性、领先-滞后结构和共现异常等模式需要多变量才能显现，而这正是标注LLM能力最薄弱之处。这两个问题造成"可靠、现实、可扩展"三难，现有方法无法同时满足三者。

Method: 将感知与描述解耦：确定性代码从真实开源多元时间序列中计算统计量，LLM负责将这些预计算的事实言语化。感知（LLM不擅长的部分）由计算处理，LLM只负责表达。基于此构建CGTime，一个4B参数的计算接地时间序列-语言模型。

Result: 在多元时间序列理解任务上，CGTime在保留测试集上取得最佳多元事实分数（0.283），超越GPT-4o-mini（0.173）和GPT-5.4-nano（0.203），差距通过Holm校正配对显著性检验得到验证。在生成标题中陈述可验证数值事实的准确度更高，覆盖的统计属性范围更广。

Conclusion: 通过计算接地的方式将感知与描述解耦，可以有效规避LLM在时间序列感知上的能力瓶颈，在较少的参数量下实现更优的多元时间序列理解与描述生成性能，为解决三难问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [287] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR)框架，将3D感知与空间推理显式解耦——利用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化的3D证据，再用LoRA微调的大语言模型（LLM）仅基于这些显式几何证据进行推理。该框架无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具使用策略，即可在主流空间推理基准上取得有竞争力的性能，同时具备更好的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端方法通过大规模训练隐式地联合获取3D感知和推理能力，作者观察到现代感知模型擅长估计连续3D几何，而LLM擅长组合与符号推理，因此希望利用两者互补优势，探索将感知与推理显式分离的替代范式。

Method: DiSR框架包含两个关键步骤：(1) 使用现成的专家感知模型（off-the-shelf expert perception models）将物理世界重建为结构化的3D证据；(2) 使用LoRA对LLM进行微调，使模型仅基于这些显式几何证据进行空间推理，无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂的工具使用策略。

Result: 在流行的空间推理基准上取得了与现有方法竞争力相当的性能，同时展现出更好的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是空间智能中可扩展且有效的替代范式，相比端到端建模更具优势，为空间推理提供了新的研究思路。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [288] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个重要问题：因子化生成模型仅通过将风格潜变量z_s的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验来诱导风格与类别信息的独立性，但这种解释是错误的。作者通过精确分解证明，边缘分布匹配只是因子化采样的四个必要条件之一，消除了风格与标签间的边缘分布不匹配既不是充分条件，甚至不足以保证因子化。实验表明多个模型全局MMD接近零，但线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签；四种缓解策略和两种条件化方法只能部分改善。结论是无法仅通过风格潜变量的边缘分布散度来证明类别独立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型通常通过将风格潜变量z_s的边缘分布正则化为固定高斯先验来声称风格表示与类别信息无关，但作者认为这种解释在数学上是不成立的。边缘分布匹配只约束了总体分布，完全没有对类别条件分布施加任何约束，因此风格潜变量即使边缘分布呈完美高斯形状，也可能高度预测类别标签。作者旨在从理论上和实验上揭示这一常见实践的缺陷。

Method: 1) 理论层面：推导出精确分解，证明边缘分布匹配仅是因子化采样所需的四个条件之一，且消除该不匹配是必要但不充分的条件。2) 实验层面：使用一个案例研究模型和四个代表性潜变量基线，评估全局MMD、线性探针准确率、聚类准确率和外部评估的条件生成效果。3) 测试六种独立扰动（模型容量、课程策略、先验几何、监督程度、两个数据集）。4) 提出并评估四种缓解策略，以及两种后处理方案：条件化先验和基于经验风格库的方法。

Result: 1) 所有测试模型全局MMD接近零，但线性探针能以74%-100%准确率恢复类别标签（10%为随机水平），案例模型聚类准确率达99.15%，而外部评估的条件生成成功率仅16%。2) 六种扰动下泄漏现象依然存在。3) 四种缓解策略可将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖基本不变。4) 后处理的条件化先验在MNIST上实现条件生成成功率达0.97（无需重训练），但CIFAR-10仅0.41；经验风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 纯基于风格潜变量边缘分布的散度（如MMD）无法证明风格与类别标签的独立性，仅报告边缘统计量不足以验证因子化生成模型通常声称的性质。要真正实现因子化采样，必须施加更强的约束（如类别条件分布的控制），单一边缘正则化实践需要被重新审视。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [289] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架，通过四阶段数据合成方法生成带优先级的多模态rubric指令数据，并将模型从"按rubric被评估的生成器"转换为"遵循rubric的执行器"。仅用10K合成样本，将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，且增益可迁移到四种开源MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的多模态指令通常包含多个重要性不等的要求，但现有多模态训练数据大多将指令遵循简化为回答一个自包含的问题，缺乏对多规则、优先级感知的指令遵循能力的研究。

Method: 提出PRISM，一个四阶段数据合成框架：(1) 生成persona-任务对；(2) 前缀引导的规则集生成；(3) 质量过滤的rubric生成；(4) 结构化验证轨迹生成。同时引入PRISM-Eval评测基准，其Loose和Strict指标通过对固定标签的确定性匹配来评估，无需推理时的judge模型。

Result: 仅用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B在PRISM-Eval上的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保持通用基准的平均性能。增益可迁移到四种额外开源MLLM（涵盖dense和MoE架构）。

Conclusion: 结构化rubric监督是通往多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令遵循的可扩展路径，为弥合现实多要求指令与现有训练数据之间的差距提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [290] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroSFT是一种参数高效的多任务SFT框架，将msft中基于全模型检查点的状态保存改为紧凑的可合并adapter状态，冻结预训练骨干网络并向adapter注入可回滚的低秩权重因子，在五个骨干网络上均取得优于msft的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务SFT将异构数据混合当作单一优化问题处理，但不同任务的最佳泛化时刻不同。msft通过任务级回滚/排除/回放来解决此问题，但其状态以全模型检查点形式物化，导致阶段切换时的存储、恢复和部署成本高昂。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练骨干网络，仅训练注入的adapter；在任务级性能峰值处回滚adapter检查点并继续训练剩余活跃任务混合。在层级别，每个adapter采用AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层作用于低秩权重因子（而非样本表示），使更新保持对输入线性、秩受限且可精确合并回冻结投影层。

Result: 在保留骨干网络对比协议下，AuroSFT平均准确率达61.36%，高于msft参考行的59.85%，并在全部五个骨干网络上均取得更高准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT证明将msft的过拟合感知调度状态压缩为紧凑可合并的adapter状态是可行且高效的，在降低状态存储/恢复/部署成本的同时提升了多任务SFT的最终性能。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [291] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroOFT是一种增强量化正交微调(qoft)的方法，通过在保持qoft作为稳定量化兼容分支的基础上，为每个适配线性层附加一个零初始化的门控低秩非线性残差分支，使模型能够进行输入相关的非线性校正。在Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B模型上，相比匹配的qoft提升Macro-6指标1.30-2.70%，相比QLoRA提升6.52-10.62%，同时节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qoft虽然实现了低比特语言模型的参数高效适配，但其任务特定更新被限制在线性正交变换范围内，缺乏输入依赖的非线性校正能力，这限制了模型对复杂任务的适配表达能力。

Method: AuroOFT采用双分支架构：保留qoft作为稳定的量化兼容线性正交分支，同时为每个被适配的线性层附加一个零起始门控低秩非线性残差分支。该分支将激活映射到RMS归一化的紧凑隐空间，使用自适应非线性基函数配合有界或token依赖的门控机制。零初始化的上投影确保AuroOFT在初始化时与qoft功能完全等价，而正交性仅作为分支级稳定性属性而非组合非线性层的属性。

Result: 在匹配数据、优化、解码和解析协议下，AuroOFT相比匹配的qoft在Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B设置上Macro-6提升1.30-2.70%，超过QLoRA 6.52-10.62%，并在代表性规模下相比QLoRA节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。

Conclusion: AuroOFT通过引入零起始非线性残差分支，成功突破了qoft的线性变换限制，在提升低比特语言模型适配性能的同时保持了对qoft的参数效率和量化兼容性优势，并显著超越QLoRA。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [292] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向散户投资者的个性化税务感知智能投顾系统，包含FastAPI后端和Web仪表盘，采用三阶段强化学习系统（自监督跨资产编码器、含意图路由的MoE分配策略、LoRA适配器），实现端到端券商集成，并如实报告其处于预部署阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散户投资者缺乏机构客户享有的个性化、税务感知的资产组合管理服务——现有机器人投顾仅使用静态规则分配，而机构级系统对个人投资者设有账户门槛和技术门槛。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建了一个功能完整的应用：前端让用户用自然语言描述投资目标，路由到六种投资授权之一，再由三阶段强化学习系统生成实时的券商集成投资组合推荐——包括自监督跨资产编码器、带学习意图路由的MoE分配策略、以及基于个人券商历史行为的LoRA适配器（免重训的个性化）。系统经端到端集成测试（Alpaca纸面交易），含多用户认证、预览-确认流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计操作链。

Result: 系统功能完备且通过端到端集成测试，但尚未对真实用户开放。提供了14天滚动前视回测（含自助置信区间）作为初步预部署验证，而非生产级性能指标。

Conclusion: 如实报告该应用为预部署阶段、具有明确的部署路径；同时总结了若干可推广的工程经验——静默失效的集成路径、挂起的第三方API调用，以及端到端实证验证优于轻信检查点元数据——这些经验对其他构建在外部实时数据源上的应用RL系统具有普适意义。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [293] [Evaluating Machine Learning Models for Post-Wildfire Debris-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05265)
*Quinn Ledingham,Zhengsen Xu,Yimin Zhu,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Motasem Alkayid,Megan Greenwood,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN）在野火后泥石流预测中的表现，发现TabPFN取得了最高的未增强性能（威胁分数0.637），并通过SHAP分析识别出短时降雨强度和风暴累积是最关键的特征，同时验证了TabPFN生成的合成数据增强能够提升除CNN外所有模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火后泥石流预测对减少暴雨期间对社区和基础设施的危害至关重要，但现有挑战包括：特征空间中泥石流事件与非泥石流事件重叠导致难以区分、机器学习模型可解释性不足、以及训练数据有限。本文旨在系统性地应对这些挑战。

Method: 利用美国西部流域尺度的野火后泥石流观测数据，对比15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN），采用重复分层交叉验证评估预测性能；使用SHAP进行特征重要性分析；使用TabPFN生成合成样本进行数据增强实验。

Result: TabPFN在未增强情况下取得最高威胁分数0.637，略优于最佳树模型；SHAP显示短时降雨强度和风暴累积排名最高，而燃烧严重度和地形特征贡献较小；合成数据增强使除CNN外的所有模型性能提升，深度学习模型平均威胁分数提升最大（+0.041）。

Conclusion: 通过严格的模型基准测试、可解释的特征分析和合成数据增强，本文提出了一个全面的框架来改善野火后泥石流预测，为相关领域的模型选择和数据稀缺问题提供了有价值的实践指导。

Abstract: Prediction of post-wildfire debris flows is critical for mitigating hazards to communities, infrastructure, and resources during intense rainfall in recently burned areas. However, identifying reliable machine learning models is complicated by overlapping debris-flow and non-debris-flow events in feature space, the need for model interpretability, and limited training data. This paper addresses these challenges through a systematic evaluation of machine learning models in terms of predictive performance, feature importance, and synthetic data augmentation. Using basin-scale observations of post-wildfire debris-flow events across the western United States, we compare 15 models, including the Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN). Repeated stratified cross-validation shows that TabPFN achieves the highest unaugmented performance with a threat score of 0.637, closely followed by the best tree-based models. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) are used to identify the features driving predictions, revealing that short-duration rainfall intensity and storm accumulation consistently rank highest, while burn severity and terrain features contribute less. We further evaluate synthetic data augmentation using TabPFN-generated samples to address the scarcity of debris-flow observations. Synthetic augmentation improves the performance of all models except CNN, with the largest mean threat score increase of +0.041 among the deep learning models. By combining rigorous model benchmarking, interpretable feature analysis, and synthetic data augmentation, this work provides a comprehensive framework for improving post-wildfire debris-flow prediction.

</details>


### [294] [Rectifying Geometric Misalignment: Online Source-Free Adaptation for Class-Imbalanced EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05315)
*Shiwen Chu,Shanglin Li,Motoaki Kawanabe,Reinmar Kobler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSPDIM，一个面向EEG-BCI的源无关在线无监督域适应框架，通过流形约束的偏置参数和信息最大化优化，解决在线脑机接口场景中因类别分布失衡（标签漂移）导致的标准黎曼对齐方法失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于EEG的BCI系统常需无监督域适应（UDA）来跨受试者和会话泛化。现有的黎曼对齐方法（如RCT）虽然能处理协变量偏移，但隐含假设类别先验均衡。实际在线BCI场景中标签分布动态变化（标签漂移），导致标准对齐技术几何上错误对齐目标数据分布。

Method: 提出OSPDIM（Online SPD manifold information maximization）框架，将流形约束的偏置参数引入切空间映射，通过信息最大化优化来纠正不平衡数据流引起的几何偏移；与依赖全局批统计的离线方法不同，OSPDIM在线逐帧估计和纠正几何偏置。

Result: 在2D SPD矩阵模拟中直观展示了OSPDIM能成功纠正标准居中方法失败的错位；在多个运动想象数据集上，OSPDIM显著优于标准黎曼基线，尤其是在严重类别不平衡的在线适应挑战性场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: OSPDIM为实际即插即用的BCI系统提供了鲁棒的解决方案，有效解决了在线适应场景中标签漂移导致的几何错位问题。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) based Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) often require unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) to generalize across subjects and sessions. While Riemannian alignment methods like the Riemannian Centering Transformation (RCT) are effective for handling covariate shifts, they implicitly assume balanced class priors. However, in realistic online BCI scenarios, the label distributions vary dynamically (label shift), causing standard alignment techniques to geometrically misalign the target data distributions. In this work, we propose OSPDIM (Online SPD manifold information maximization), a source-free online UDA framework designed to address label shifts on the Riemannian manifold. OSPDIM introduces a manifold-constrained bias parameter into the tangent space mapping, which is optimized via information maximization to correct the geometric skew caused by imbalanced data streams. Unlike offline methods relying on global batch statistics, OSPDIM estimates and corrects geometric bias on-the-fly. Simulations on 2D SPD matrices visually demonstrate that OSPDIM successfully rectifies the misalignment where standard centering fails. Extensive experiments on multiple motor imagery datasets show that OSPDIM significantly outperforms standard Riemannian baselines, particularly in challenging online adaptation scenarios with severe class imbalance, offering a robust solution for practical, plug-and-play BCI systems.

</details>


### [295] [QEvict: Recoverable Quantized KV Eviction for Attention-Drift-Robust Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05326)
*Ayushman Garg,Akshita Gupta,Shaswata Bhattacharya,Abhishek Gupta,Sandeep Kumar,Manoj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QEvict，一种三层KV缓存管理方案，将传统的二值化删除策略改为可恢复的驱逐机制，通过量化恢复层保留中间重要性的窗口，解决了自回归推理中token被永久驱逐后无法恢复的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的KV缓存驱逐策略基于注意力分数做二值化决策（保留或删除），这一决策是不可逆的——token一旦被驱逐就永久丢失。但研究发现，在解码过程中，token和窗口的重要性会随着生成查询的变化而漂移，导致标准驱逐策略会永久丢弃那些在完整缓存模型下后来会获得大量注意力的状态。因此需要一种可恢复的驱逐机制来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出两种诊断指标：Future Missed Mass（衡量被丢弃状态未来获得的注意力）和Global LIR（衡量历史非活跃区域的再激活）。基于此提出QEvict三层管理方案：第一层以全精度保留高置信度窗口；第二层以量化可恢复层存储中间置信度窗口；第三层仅删除最低置信度窗口。解码时通过累积注意力分数更新窗口重要性，被重新激活的量化窗口会反量化并提升至全精度层。

Result: 在固定内存预算下，QEvict比传统驱逐策略和量化基线保留了更广泛的历史上下文，同时为最重要的区域保持精确的全精度。在长上下文理解、检索和推理基准上，QEvict一致优于代表性的驱逐基线和量化基线，减少了被遗漏的注意力并提高了信息保留率。

Conclusion: QEvict证明了将驱逐问题从二值化的"保留或删除"转变为三层的"可恢复驱逐"是可行且有效的，在固定内存预算下既保留了更完整的历史上下文，又不牺牲最重要区域的全精度，为KV缓存管理提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model inference is increasingly constrained by the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache. A dominant line of work reduces this footprint by evicting tokens that appear unimportant under attention-derived scores. However, such policies make an implicit irreversible decision: once a token is evicted, it cannot become useful again. We show that this assumption is brittle during decoding. Token and window importance drift as generated queries evolve, causing standard eviction policies to permanently discard states that later receive substantial attention under the full-cache model. To characterize this behaviour, we introduce Future Missed Mass and Global LIR, two diagnostics that measure future attention assigned to discarded states and the reactivation of historically inactive regions. We propose QEvict, a three-tier KV-cache management scheme that replaces binary retain-or-delete eviction with recoverable eviction. QEvict maintains high-confidence windows in full precision, stores intermediate windows in a quantized recoverable tier, and deletes only the lowest-confidence windows. During decoding, cumulative attention scores update window importance and when a quantized window becomes important again, it is dequantized and promoted to the full-precision. Under a fixed memory budget, this design preserves broader historical context while retaining exact full precision for the most important regions. Across long-context understanding, retrieval, and reasoning benchmarks, QEvict consistently improves over representative eviction and quantization baselines, reducing missed attention and improving information retention

</details>


### [296] [DG-FedReuse: Proxy-Gradient-Gated Cached-Update Reuse with Matched Sparse Uplink Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05358)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-FedReuse机制，允许选定的客户端在随机头梯度差异代理低于轮次相关阈值时，贡献带年龄衰减的缓存更新。在90轮预算下实现83.36-85.42%的上行更新数据字段节省（对比Top-K FedAvg的76.88%），但精度差异为-5.29至-0.14个百分点。考虑到称重下行灵敏度后，节省降至41.68-42.71%，增益仅3.24-4.27个百分点。作者明确声明该方法不建立无偏泛化、端到端带宽降低、运行时或能量节省等结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习频繁进行本地优化和模型更新传输，通信开销是主要瓶颈。现有Top-K稀疏化等压缩方法虽能降低上行通信，但未充分利用历史缓存更新，且缺乏对更新重用时机和有效性的系统性研究。作者旨在提出一种模拟器级机制，通过合理重用过期缓存更新来进一步提升上行通信效率。

Method: DG-FedReuse包含三个核心组件：(1)重用规则——当随机头梯度差异代理低于轮次相关阈值时，允许选定客户端贡献年龄衰减的缓存更新；(2)重用约束——设置硬缓存年龄上限和最小新鲜客户端配额；(3)新鲜更新压缩——使用自适应逐张量Top-K数值字段表示。实验在50个虚拟客户端、Dirichlet标签异质性(α=0.5)和三个种子上进行。

Result: 在90轮预算下，DG-FedReuse实现83.36-85.42%的更新数据字段上行节省（对比Top-K FedAvg的76.88%），但种子对齐精度差异为-5.29至-0.14个百分点。最佳测试精度在-2.38至+0.45个百分点区间。当考虑对称密集模型下行灵敏度后，总节省降至41.68-42.71%，相对Top-K FedAvg的增量增益为3.24-4.27个百分点。

Conclusion: DG-FedReuse在模拟器层面展示了缓存更新重用在通信压缩方面的潜力，但作者明确限制结论范围：该研究仅表征了模拟器中提出的重用规则，不建立无偏泛化、端到端带宽降低、运行时或能量节省、更快收敛或对现有过期更新/惰性聚合方法的优越性。通信结论高度依赖于核算边界（是否计入下行通信）。

Abstract: Federated learning repeatedly incurs local optimization and model-update transmission. We study DG-FedReuse, a simulator-level mechanism that allows selected clients to contribute age-decayed cached updates when a stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy remains below a round-dependent threshold. A hard cache-age limit and minimum fresh-client quota constrain reuse, while fresh updates use an adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation. Experiments cover six image-classification datasets, 50 virtual clients, Dirichlet label heterogeneity (α=0.5), and three seeds. At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [297] [Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs) for Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05371)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Jianfeng Zhu,Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量子结构化世界模型（QSWMs），一种受量子理论启发的世界建模框架，使用复值表示和密度矩阵式潜在状态，在元胞自动机上初步验证了其预测能力，但长时程推演和密度矩阵变体存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型多以经典向量、概率分布、循环隐状态或Transformer激活表示潜在状态，缺乏对更丰富数学结构（如量子理论中的复值表示与密度矩阵）作为世界建模归纳偏置的系统研究。

Method: 提出QSWM框架，包含结构化潜在状态、潜在转移算子和测量启发的解码映射；建立经典包含性、预测充分性和结构化紧凑性三个基础性质；实例化复值QSWM和密度矩阵式QSWM两种变体。

Result: 在基本元胞自动机上与强经典基线对比，复值QSWM展现出有前景的局部预测潜力，但长时程推演和密度矩阵变体性能存在局限。

Conclusion: 量子启发的数学结构为世界模型提供了值得探索的归纳偏置，但当前变体在长时程预测和密度矩阵表示方面仍面临挑战，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: World models learn latent states that summarize interaction histories, evolve over time, and support prediction, simulation, or planning. Most existing world models represent these states using classical vectors, probability distributions, recurrent hidden states, or transformer activations. In this paper, we introduce Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs), a quantum-inspired framework for predictive world modeling with structured latent states, latent transition operators, and measurement-inspired decoding maps. We study whether mathematical structures inspired by quantum theory, such as complex-valued representations and density-matrix-like latents, provide useful inductive biases for world modeling. We establish three foundational properties: classical inclusion, predictive sufficiency, and structured compactness. We then instantiate complex-valued and density-matrix-like QSWM variants and evaluate them on elementary cellular automata against strong classical baselines. Results show promising local predictive potential for complex-valued QSWMs, while also revealing limitations in long-horizon rollout, density-matrix variants

</details>


### [298] [Spectral Distillation: From Nonlinear Dynamics to Linear State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05416)
*Liane Galanti,Devan Shah,Shlomo Fortgang,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可证明的端到端流水线，通过凸学习和可证明蒸馏来从非线性动力系统观测中提取紧致线性状态空间（LDS）表示，无需直接求解非凸的系统识别问题。核心贡献是证明了蒸馏LDS的平均预测误差分解为指数小的蒸馏项和由Luenberger复杂度决定的OSF学习项，且保证与维度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上从非线性动力系统学习线性表示需要求解非凸的系统识别问题，缺乏保证且计算困难。本文旨在回答一个核心问题：能否通过紧凑线性状态空间表示学习非线性动力学，同时避免直接求解非凸问题，并提供端到端可证明的保障。

Method: 两阶段流水线：（1）使用观测谱滤波（OSF）这一凸方法从非线性系统观测中学习一个隐式谱预测器，其性能可与最佳线性观测器竞争；（2）通过谱到LDS蒸馏（spectral-to-LDS distillation）将该隐式预测器转换为显式的循环线性动力系统。理论分析将蒸馏LDS的预测误差分解为指数小的蒸馏项加OSF学习项。

Result: 主要定理表明蒸馏LDS的平均预测误差由指数小的蒸馏项和受Luenberger复杂度约束的OSF学习项组成，且该保证是维度无关的（仅依赖观测器复杂度而非表示非线性系统所需的潜在维度）。实验在线性LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆任务上表明，训练-蒸馏流水线生成的紧凑LDS预测器与直接训练的基线相当或更优。

Conclusion: 本文提供了首个端到端可证明的方法，通过凸学习加可证明蒸馏从非线性动力学中提取最佳事后（best-in-hindsight）LDS表示，兼具理论保证和实际有效性。

Abstract: Can nonlinear dynamical systems be learned through a compact linear state-space representation, without directly solving a non-convex system-identification problem? We give a provable pipeline for doing so. Starting from observations of an unknown nonlinear dynamical system, we first learn an implicit spectral predictor using Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF), a convex method that competes with the best linear observer for the system. We then apply spectral-to-LDS distillation to convert this predictor into an explicit recurrent linear dynamical system. Our main theorem shows that the average prediction error of the distilled LDS decomposes into an exponentially-small distillation term and the OSF learning term governed by the Luenberger complexity of the best observer. The guarantee is dimension-free: it depends on observer complexity rather than on the latent dimension needed to represent the nonlinear system. To our knowledge, this yields the first end-to-end provable method for extracting a best-in-hindsight LDS representation of nonlinear dynamics through convex learning followed by provable distillation. Experiments on linear LDS benchmarks and MuJoCo behavior cloning show that the train-then-distill pipeline produces compact LDS predictors that match or outperform directly trained baselines.

</details>


### [299] [Perturbation Sensitivity at Convergence: A Simple Signal for Identifying Spuriously Correlated Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05419)
*Nilesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需分组标注即可识别受虚假相关性影响的样本的方法。与以往依赖训练早期信号的方法不同，本文发现收敛后的模型仍存在可用信号：与虚假相关性一致的样本由共享规则分类，而其余样本则通过个别输入特定配置拟合，更易受扰动影响。通过在收敛模型输入上施加固定扰动，可将两类样本区分开来。该方法仅需每个训练样本两次前向传播，无需组标注和早停轮次，在Waterbirds上将最差组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%（基真标注为85.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验风险最小化训练的模型在含有虚假相关性的数据上虽平均精度高，但在相关不成立的子群体上表现差。现有无需组标注的识别方法依赖于训练早期的信号，需要提前定位介入的epoch，而该超参数通常需用带组标注的验证集选择，这限制了其应用。

Method: 核心洞察是：在收敛后，损失无法区分两个群体时依然存在可用信号——与虚假相关性一致的样本由共享的分类规则（shortcut）决定，而其余样本通过针对个别输入的特定配置拟合，后者更"脆弱"。因此，对收敛模型的输入施加一个固定扰动，被虚假相关性影响的样本预测翻转的频率远低于未受影响样本。该过程只需每个训练样本两次前向传播，无需组标注和早停轮次。

Result: 使用检测到的样本重新平衡训练，在Waterbirds上将最差组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，而使用基真组标签时为85.8%，差距很小。

Conclusion: 收敛后的模型输入扰动可作为一种可靠的信号来识别虚假相关性影响的样本，无需早期训练信号的介入时机选择和组标注，有效提升最差组准确率，且性能接近使用基真标签的上限。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [300] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了信息流网络（IFNs），将生成式流网络框架（AFlowNets）扩展到不完全信息博弈中，并证明了原有约束在不完全信息博弈中无法获得有效密度（对应玩家策略）和有效训练目标，通过泛化该框架严格推广了AFlowNets，在三个标准博弈环境中表现出与OSMCCFR和标准RL方法相当或更优的性能与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽已大量探索将强化学习（RL）与反事实遗憾（CFR）方法结合以权衡计算速度与性能，但生成式采样框架在不完全信息博弈中的应用研究相对匮乏，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文将对抗流网络（AFlowNets）这一生成式流网络框架扩展到不完全信息博弈场景，提出信息流网络（IFNs）。首先证明了原有的生成流网络约束在获取有效密度（对应玩家策略）和有效训练目标时不可行，进而提出IFlowNets作为其泛化版本，严格扩展了AFlowNets框架。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境中的初步结果显示，IFlowNets在性能和速度上与Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret（OSMCCFR）以及标准RL方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: IFlowNets成功地将生成式流网络方法应用于不完全信息博弈，通过严格泛化AFlowNets解决了原框架在该场景下的约束失效问题，实验验证了其竞争力，为该领域提供了新的生成式采样研究途径。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [301] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"自由"（freedom）才是探索式建模（XM）真正的第三个预训练轴，而非生成表现力（generative expressivity）。XM通过产生K个候选输出并选择最接近的一个进行更新，将候选错失可接受区域的概率提升至K次幂。作者从理论上证明平均XM损失取决于该错失概率，并实证表明XM优化的正是自由（行为约束的弱化程度），且自由选择器在30个案例中赢下29个，优于MDL达110-500%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生成式训练中，每轮仅用一个输出来比较，导致模型倾向于产出单一常见答案而丧失更广的生成多样性。XM引入K个候选输出的探索机制作为"第三预训练轴"，但作者认为该轴的本质是自由（行为约束的弱化），而非生成表现力。此前的形式化工作已证明自由是函数属性而非形式属性，且最弱的模型最可能泛化。本文旨在理论证明并实证验证XM是在优化自由。

Method: 作者先给出形式化证明：平均XM损失依赖于候选错失可接受区域的概率，探索将该概率提升至K次幂；当K>1时，匹配概率随自由增加而上升。随后进行两组实证：(1) Forward XM实验，测量在更大K下自由是否增加或饱和，并测试上下文依赖目标下的自由变化；(2) 训练XM候选池，用读取无标签父上下文的自由选择器与验证集选择对比，在分布漂移下评估。
